<script data-dashboard-update="patch.js">
/* 只补本SKU、林和精确映射、美国已履约PO的成本模型；不替换零售价或已存在完整利润。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const data = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('inventoryServiceProfitData').textContent);
  const model = root.ppProfitModel, core = root.orderMarginCore;
  if (!model?.ready || !core || data.status !== 'verified') throw Error('Wayfair同SKU利润补证未就绪');
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const esc = value => cxEsc(String(value ?? ''));
  const records = new Map(data.rows.map(row => [row.sku, row]));
  const storage = new Map(data.storageRows.map(row => [row.key, row]));
  const seen = new Set(), samples = [];
  for (const sample of data.samples) {
    const ident = [sample.platform,sample.store,sample.orderId,sample.sku,sample.shopSku].join('|');
    if (seen.has(ident) || sample.platform !== 'Wayfair' || sample.store !== 'way_linhe'
      || !records.has(sample.sku) || sample.sourceSku !== sample.sku
      || sample.quantity <= 0 || sample.revenue <= 0 || sample.eligibleForMargin !== true
      || !sample.withoutModelReserve || !sample.warehouseProof?.length
      || !records.get(sample.sku).allowedWarehouses.includes(sample.warehouse)
      || sample.warehouseProof.some(row=>row.warehouse!==sample.warehouse)
      || Math.abs(sample.operatingPurchase-sample.truePurchase*1.5)>1e-7)
      throw Error('新增利润样本身份未闭合：'+ident);
    const costs = ['head','tariff','handling','tail','platformFee','advertising','consumptionTax','installation'];
    if (!costs.every(key=>finite(sample.componentTotals[key]))) throw Error('新增利润费用缺失：'+ident);
    const common = costs.reduce((sum,key)=>sum+sample.componentTotals[key],0);
    if (Math.abs(sample.revenue-common-sample.operatingPurchase*sample.quantity-sample.pureOperatingProfitTotal)>1e-7
      || Math.abs(sample.revenue-common-sample.truePurchase*sample.quantity-sample.pureTrueProfitTotal)>1e-7)
      throw Error('新增利润金额未闭合：'+ident);
    seen.add(ident); samples.push(sample);
  }
  samples.sort((a,b)=>String(b.sampleDate).localeCompare(String(a.sampleDate))
    ||String(b.orderId).localeCompare(String(a.orderId))||String(b.key).localeCompare(String(a.key)));
  function decorate(sample) {
    const row = storage.get(sample.key);
    const matched = row && row.sku===sample.sku && row.orderId===sample.orderId && row.quantity===sample.quantity;
    const fee = matched && row.complete===true && finite(row.amount) ? row.amount : null;
    const perSet = fee===null ? null : fee/sample.quantity;
    const scenario = matched ? {...row,storage:{lower:perSet,representative:perSet,upper:perSet}}
      : {complete:false,scope:'missing',scopeLabel:'本单仓储批号/逐日账单仍待核',storage:{lower:null,representative:null,upper:null}};
    return {...sample,storageScenario:scenario,storageFee:perSet,storageFeeLower:perSet,storageFeeUpper:perSet,
      warehouseFeeTotal:fee,storageOperatingProfitTotal:fee===null?null:sample.pureOperatingProfitTotal-fee,
      storageTrueProfitTotal:fee===null?null:sample.pureTrueProfitTotal-fee,
      storageOperatingMargin:fee===null?null:(sample.pureOperatingProfitTotal-fee)/sample.revenue,
      storageTrueMargin:fee===null?null:(sample.pureTrueProfitTotal-fee)/sample.revenue};
  }
  const priorForSku = model.forSku;
  model.forSku = function (sku,filters={}) {
    const base=priorForSku.call(this,sku,filters), evidence=records.get(sku);
    if (!evidence || base.selected) return base;
    const eligible=samples.filter(row=>row.sku===sku&&model.channelMatches(row,filters));
    const chosen=eligible.slice(0,20).map(decorate), historical=model.aggregate(chosen);
    const restored=new Set(eligible.map(row=>[row.platform,row.store,row.orderId].join('|')));
    const rejected=[...(base.rejected||[]),...data.rejectedSamples.filter(row=>row.sku===sku&&model.channelMatches(row,filters))]
      .filter(row=>!restored.has([row.platform,row.store,row.orderId].join('|')));
    const result={...base,historical,forecast:null,selected:historical,samples:chosen,historicalSamples:chosen,
      validSampleCount:eligible.length,rejectedSampleCount:rejected.length,rejected,
      status:historical?'Wayfair原成交 · 自身成本模型':base.status,
      isForecast:false,isEstimate:true,isOwnCostEstimate:Boolean(historical),ownBackfill:Boolean(historical),
      inventoryServiceProfit:Boolean(historical),settledProfitComplete:false,
      ownBackfillEvidence:{...evidence,missingFields:historical?[]:['当前渠道无本SKU已核林和模型样本，未跨平台/店铺借用'],
        notes:[...evidence.notes,...data.actualCostGaps.map(value=>'实结待核：'+value)]},
      noChannelSample:!historical,noCrossSkuFallback:true};
    return result;
  };
  const priorBuild=sqBuildRow;
  sqBuildRow=function(sku,filters) {
    const row=priorBuild.apply(this,arguments), profit=model.forSku(sku,filters||{}),summary=profit.selected;
    if (!profit.inventoryServiceProfit) return row;
    return {...row,_profit20:profit,operatingMargin:summary.operatingMargin,realMargin:summary.trueMargin,
      storageOperatingMargin:summary.storageOperatingMargin,storageRealMargin:summary.storageTrueMargin,
      storageProfitComplete:summary.storageComplete,profitSampleCount:summary.sampleCount,
      profitSampleStart:summary.periodStart,profitSampleEnd:summary.periodEnd,
      profitPlatformCount:summary.platformCount,profitStoreCount:summary.storeCount,profitStores:summary.stores.join('；'),
      profitBasis:'same_sku_wayfair_own_cost_model',profitStatus:profit.status,isEstimate:true,
      sourceNote:'Wayfair林和已履约PO供货收入；同SKU同具体仓齐套包件实物身份、采购与管理成本模型，非零售现价、非财务实结。'
        +'尾程平台承担0、费6%、ROI22均为场景假设；未扣5%模型售后预留；样本'+summary.periodStart+'—'+summary.periodEnd+'。'};
  };
  const priorCell=sqUiCell;
  sqUiCell=function(column,row) {
    const html=priorCell.apply(this,arguments);
    if (!row._profit20?.inventoryServiceProfit) return html;
    if(column==='operatingMargin'||column==='realMargin') return html+'<small class="pp-sub">Wayfair原供货价 · 成本模型</small>';
    if(column==='evidence')return html+'<small class="pp-sub">'+esc('林和精确保留映射；已履约，非零售预测；尾程0/费6%/ROI22为假设，实结待核')+'</small>';
    return html;
  };
  // 原费用构成和CSV继续调用同一forSku/sqBuildRow；missing筛选按新计算结果判断。
  model.clearCaches();
  const marker=document.createElement('span');marker.id='inventoryServiceProfitReady';marker.hidden=true;
  marker.dataset.sampleCount=String(samples.length);document.body.appendChild(marker);
  root.inventoryServiceProfitModel={ready:true,data,samples,decorate,forSku:model.forSku};
})(window);

</script>
